Title Path: Performance measurements for 5G network functions > Performance measurements for SMSF > MO SMS message delivery related measurements > Number of MO SMS delivery procedure requests
Content: [ a) ] This measurement provides the number of MO SMS delivery procedure requests received by the SMSF from AMF . CC SMSF receives the MO SMS delivery procedure ( POST ) operation request sent by A MF for the " /ue-contexts/\ supi\ /sendsms" resource URL (see clause 5.2.2.4 of TS 29.540 ). Each request is added to the relevant subcounter per S-NSSAI. Each subcounter is an integer value SMSF.MoReq.SNSSAI Where SNSSAI identifies the S-NSSAI; SMSF Function Valid for packet switched traffic 5GS One usage of this performance measurements is for performance assurance.
Title Path: Performance measurements for 5G network functions > Performance measurements for SMSF > MO SMS message delivery related measurements > Number of successful MO SMS delivery procedure s
Content: [ a) ] This measurement provides the number of successful MO SMS delivery procedure s at the SMSF . CC SMS F returns "200 OK" response message (see clause 5.2.2.4 of TS 29.540 ). Each successful delivery procedure is added to the relevant subcounter per S-NSSAI. Each subcounter is an integer value SMSF.MoSucc.SNSSAI Where SNSSAI identifies the S-NSSAI; SMSF Function Valid for packet switched traffic 5GS One usage of this performance measurements is for performance assurance.

Title Path: Performance measurements for 5G network functions > Performance measurements for EES > EAS Discovery procedure related measurements > EAS discovery failure
Content: [ a) ] This measurement provides the number of EAS discovery failures when EES fails to discover and select a suitable EAS under its service area. CC On transmission of EAS discovery response (see clause 8.5.2 in TS 23.558 ) by the EES to the EEC that indicates the EAS discovery failure, the measurement is obtained by incrementing the appropriate subcounters. Some the examples are shown below: - UeLocation (see table 8.5.3.2-1 in TS 23.558 ), as contained in the corresponding EAS discovery request (see clause 8.5.2 in TS 23.558 ). This will give the total number of EAS discovery Request got failed containing the given UE location. - EasType (see table 8.5.3.2-2 in TS 23.558 ), as contained in the corresponding EAS discovery request (see clause 8.5.2 in TS 23.558 ). This will give the total number of EAS discovery Request got failed containing the given EasType as EAS discovery filter. - EasSchedule (see table 8.5.3.2-2 in TS 23.558 ), as contained in the EAS Discovery request (see clause 8.5.2 in TS 23.558 ). This will give the total number of EAS discovery Request got failed containing the given EasSchedule as EAS discovery filter. This measurement is performed per EAS Discovery Filter (see table 8.5.3.2-2 in TS 23.558 ). Each subcounter is an integer value DIS.EasDisFail.UeLocation where UeLocation: identifies where the UE is connected to the network or the position of the UE (see clause 7.3.2 in TS 23.558 ), and is represented by the cell ID. DIS.EasDisFail.provider where provider is the EAS provider identifier of EAS Discovery Filter (see table 8.5.3.2-2 in TS 23.558 ). DIS.EasDisFail.id where id is the EAS identifier of EAS Discovery Filter (see table 8.5.3.2-2 in TS 23.558 ). DIS.EasDisFail.type where type is the EAS type of EAS Discovery Filter (see table 8.5.3.2-2 in TS 23.558 ). DIS.EasDisFail.schedule where schedule is the EAS schedule of EAS Discovery Filter (see table 8.5.3.2-2 in TS 23.558 ). DIS.EasDisFail.geoloc where geoloc is the EAS Geographical Service Area of EAS Discovery Filter (see table 8.5.3.2-2 in TS 23.558 ). DIS.EasDisFail.toploc where toploc is the EAS Topological Service Area of EAS Discovery Filter (see table 8.5.3.2-2 in TS 23.558 ). DIS.EasDisFail.srvCont where srvCont is the Service continuity support of EAS Discovery Filter (see table 8.5.3.2-2 in TS 23.558 ). DIS.EasDisFail.srvPerm where srvPerm is the required level of service permissions e.g. trial, gold-class of EAS Discovery Filter (see table 8.5.3.2-2 in TS 23.558 ). DIS.EasDisFail.srvFeature where srvFeature is the required service features e.g. single vs. multi-player gaming service of EAS Discovery Filter (see table 8.5.3.2-2 in TS 23.558 ). EESFunction Valid for packet switched traffic 5GS One usage of this performance measurements is for EES performance assurance.

Title Path: Performance measurements for 5G network functions > Performance measurements for NWDAF > Measurements related to the NWDAF analytics service > Time consumption of NWDAF providing analytics service information
Content: [ a) ] This measurement provides the time consumed by the NWDAF to generate and provide the analytics service information. The measurement is calculated per Analytics ID (see clause5.1.6.2.42 and 5.1.6.3.4 in TS 29.520 ). DER (n=1) This measurement is obtained by the following method: - the time when the NWDAF sends the notification related to the analytics service subscription to the NWDAF service consumer, minus the time when the NWDAF receives the corresponding analytics service subscription from the NWDAF service consumer (See TS 23.288 ). In the case where there are multiple notifications for single subscription, this measurement is obtained by calculating the time when the NWDAF sends the first notification related to the analytics service subscription to the NWDAF service consumer, minus the time when the NWDAF receives the corresponding analytics service subscription from the NWDAF service consumer, or - the time when the NWDAF sends the response related to the analytics service request to the NWDAF service consumer, minus the time when the NWDAF receives the corresponding analytics service request from the NWDAF service consumer (See TS 23.288 ). A real value in milliseconds The measurement name has the form DANS.AnalyticsSerTimeCons, and DANS.AnalyticsSerTimeCons.AnalyticsID, where AnalyticsID identifies different analytics. NWDAFFunction. Valid for packet switched traffic. 5GS.

SNSSAI , where SNSSAI identifies the S-NSSAI. NWDAFFunction Valid for packet switched traffic 5GS

CC On receipt by the NWDAF of the subscription to NWDAF service (See clause 7 in TS 23.288 ), each rejected subscription is added to the corresponding counter. The measurement can be split into subcounters per service name (see clause 4.1 in TS 29.520 ), subcounters per Analytics ID (see clause5.1.6.2.42 and 5.1.6.3.4 in TS 29.520 ) and subcounters per S-NSSAI. An integer value The measurement name has the form DANS.SerSubRejected, and DANS.SerSubRejected.ServiceName where ServiceName identifies different services, and DANS.SerSubRejected.AnalyticsID, where AnalyticsID identifies different analytics, and DANS.SerSubRejected. SNSSAI , where SNSSAI identifies the S-NSSAI. NWDAFFunction Valid for packet switched traffic 5GS

Title Path: Performance measurements for 5G network functions > Performance measurements for NWDAF > NWDAF ML Model related measurements > Usage of ML models in NWDAF > Distribution of ML models usage in NWDAF
Content: [ a) ] This measurement provides the distribution of usage of ML models in NWDAF to generate and provide analytics for a specific time interval. This measurement is applicable when ML models are stored in the NWDAF rather than in a central repository CC This measurement is obtained by the following method: - for each ML model its usage is determined for a specific time interval; - the bin with the range corresponding to the ML model usage is selected; - the value of the counter for the selected bin is incremented by 1 E.g., for a ML model that was used 25 times to generate and provide analytics between time t and t-60 minutes, the counter corresponding to the bin "15-30" is incremented by one Each measurement is an integer representing the count of ML model usage within the range of the bin The measurement name has the form DANS.MLModelUsageCount.Bin where Bin indicates the size range which is vendor specific NWDAFFunction Valid for packet switching 5GS

This measurement is applicable when ML models are stored in the NWDAF rather than in a central repository SI This measurement is obtained by inspecting the usage of ML models in NWDAF and then taking their arithmetic mean A single integer value The measurement name has the form DANS. Content: [ a) ] This measurement provides the number of ML model training events in which the "Maximum response time'' is lower than the "Time when ML model training information is delivered to the consumer" and "Time when ML model training information is delivered to the consumer" is lower than the ``Extended maximum response time''. When the ``Maximum response time'' is lower than the "Time when ML model training information is delivered to the consumer" and "Time when ML model training information is delivered to the consumer" is lower than the ``Extended maximum response time'', the related event will be added to the corresponding counter. Valid for packet switched traffic. 5GS.
Title Path: Measurements related to end-to-end 5G network and network slicing > Virtualised resource usage measurement
Content: a) This measurement provides the mean usage of virtualised resource (e.g. processor, memory, disk) in single network slice instance during the granularity period. b) OM c) This measurement is generated with.sum suffix for the usage of each virtualised NF (see TS 32.426 ) related to single network slice instance by taking the weighted average. The algorithm of the weighted average is vendor specific. d) Each measurement is an real value (Unit:\%). e) MeanProcessorUsage MeanMemoryUsage MeanDiskUsage f) Performance measurement service. g) Packet Switched. h) 5GS NOTE: The name of service in f) needs to align with the TS (e.g., 28.550) defining the management service.
